# 工程总承包（EPC／DB）争议解决实战攻略

> 张旗 叶海 余灵颖 · 微信读书 · epub

**简介**：本书系三位建设工程领域的年轻律师在实践工作中总结出的工作成果，既有风险管理应对策略，又有针对具体案件争议的分析。特别是书中基于真实争议案例的分析论证既说明了真实问题的复杂性，又展现了专业律师解决现实问题的逻辑与思路;而且不仅仅是法律问题的争锋，书中实务建议部分还有三位著者工程管理思维的体现。相信本书对于EPC实务工作者能有所助益。

## 序一

做建设工程案件的专职律师很多，但研究建筑工程实务问题的很少，我带的几百名博士硕士中，好像只有三个既挣钱又出成果的，一个是浙江大公律师事务所的余卫博士，一个是广西同望律师事务所的李彩凤硕士，还有一个是泰和泰（深圳）律师事务所的张旗硕士。

我国是基建大国，建筑工程纠纷多，标的大，当事人大多有支付能力，历来是律师的兵家必争之地。但是，建筑工程纠纷的水很深，深到足以让律师喘着大气仰望星空的程度。无所不在的阴阳施工合同、无可奈何的层层转包分包、无计可施的虚假工程签证、无奇不有的工程造价鉴定，还有无理可争的群体性事件，全是无人能解的迷宫。没有一点勇气，没有一点技术，没有一点阅历，真不敢涉足。

张旗的团队准备出书了，书名是《工程总承包(EPC/DB)争议解决实战攻略》。年轻人最大的资本是无畏，实战攻略是将军的口气。这本书是否真有将军的雄才大略和三十六计，自有读者评论，但其中的工程管理和法律逻辑的思维碰撞、建筑工程纠纷实例的庖丁解牛般的复盘分析、办案过程中积累而成的经验领悟，对于律师和准律师的法学院学生有显而易见的阅读价值。

是为序。

![](images/3392c82a8d13.jpg)

2022年1月26日于海口

## 序二 解案析法 拨沙见金

序二

### 序二　解案析法　拨沙见金

工程总承包是近些年建筑业最热门的词汇。

2016年始，国务院、发改委、住建部等政府部门陆续出台《关于进一步推进工程总承包发展的若干意见》（建市〔2016〕93号）、《关于促进建筑业持续健康发展的意见》（国办发〔2017〕19号）、《关于加快新型建筑工业化发展的若干意见》（建标规〔2020〕8号）、《房屋建筑和市政基础设施项目工程总承包管理办法》（建市规〔2019〕12号）等相关政策、制度，积极推行工程总承包模式，倡导设计、采购、施工深度融合，以提高工程建设质量和效益。实践中，交通、水利、能源、化工、机场、港口等建设领域更是将工程总承包作为工程承发包的主要交易模式。

但与工程总承包的发展趋势不相匹配的是，现阶段工程总承包的规则制度供给不足。工程总承包的专项法律规定尚未出台，《民法典》《建筑法》等法律规定仅能提供原则参考，无法有针对性地适用于工程总承包领域。而传统的施工总承包与工程总承包差异较大，施工合同相关管理理念亦不能套用于工程总承包。陷入制度缺失的困境，工程总承包实施过程中出现了大量的争议。

鉴于此，泰和泰深圳办公室张旗、叶海、余灵颖律师复盘了大量的工程总承包项目法律服务案例，提炼整理工程总承包全过程法律服务指引，梳理工程总承包领域常见的法律纠纷，并运用“法律+造价+项目管理”的综合思维，总结出参考价值极强的工作建议，对化解工程总承包合同争议、解决项目管理实践问题大有裨益。

众所周知，建设工程领域专业性强，对律师的要求也高。作为泰和泰律师事务所的首席合伙人，我很欣喜地看到优秀的泰和泰青年律师迎难而上，不断思考总结、复盘并形成内容输出。

“君子之道，辟如行远必自迩，辟如登高必自卑。”希望张旗、叶海、余灵颖律师坚持长期主义、专业主义，持续探究工程总承包模式的有序运行，持续探讨工程总承包建筑领域法律专业服务，为建筑业健康发展贡献力量。

是为序。

![](images/118638839f09.jpg)

法学博士

泰和泰律师事务所首席合伙人

第十届中华全国律师协会副会长

2022年1月

## 自序

2016年5月20日，住建部发布《关于进一步推进工程总承包发展的若干意见》（建市〔2016〕93号），从工程总承包的意义、模式、管理制度等方面进行了规定，并规定政府投资项目和装配式建筑应当积极采用工程总承包模式。随后，国务院办公厅于2017年2月21日发布《关于促进建筑业持续健康发展的意见》（国办发〔2017〕19号），要求装配式建筑原则上应采用工程总承包模式。政府投资工程应完善建设管理模式，带头推行工程总承包。以上述政策为开端，国家不断出台政策性文件鼓励工程建设主体采用工程总承包模式，并在房屋建筑和市政基础设施领域得到极大推广。

与传统的施工总承包模式（设计—招标—建造〈Design-Bid-Build〉模式）相比，工程总承包模式(EPC/DB)具有简化招标程序、缩短建设周期、降低工程造价、强化质量责任、降低发包人管理投入等一系列优势，在当下的工程建设中备受推崇。工程总承包项目的法律服务已成为建设工程法律领域的新热点。

无可讳言的是，工程总承包模式的实施效果并不理想，法律争议频发。主要原因是，工程总承包模式缺乏专门立法，仅有散落在《建筑法》和《民法典》中的若干原则性条文规定，在实践中缺乏普遍的指导性。在司法裁判领域，工程总承包模式也缺乏可资援引的基本规则。特别是工程总承包模式推广至复杂多变的房屋建筑领域之后，由于市场参与主体合同管理水平参差不齐，计价规则和风险分配条款等法律造价交叉内容极易引发争议。

乘着工程总承包的风口，笔者团队曾为多个大型工程总承包项目提供法律服务，如横琴新区海绵城市第一批示范项目(EPC)、横琴科学城（一期）勘察设计施工总承包项目（105.4亿元）、美的库卡智能制造产业园一期EPC项目等，并开展了多项贯穿“项目策划+项目管理+争议解决”项目建设全过程的法律服务，处理了多项争议，积累了一定经验。

本书创作的初衷是将笔者团队开展工程总承包项目法律服务案件（包括诉讼、非诉项目）的经验进行全面复盘和提升。具体篇章的创作均以实际案例、常见问题为切入点，针对近年来对工程总承包模式(EPC/DB)在国内推行过程中产生的典型争议，从律师办案和实务处理视角进行分析。笔者团队力争本书更具有实践价值，能为各位工程总承包参与者实际问题的解决提供参考。

在本书第一章，笔者首先以某EPC项目全过程法律服务的经验为基础，梳理总结了笔者团队为EPC项目发包人提供全过程法律服务的工作指引。同时，对“EPC+O”“PPP+EPC”等模式从非诉实务的角度进行了专门论述，提出了建设性意见。

在第二章“争议解决”中选取真实案例，从律师团队办案视角出发，条分缕析解读争议问题所涉的工程管理、工程计价、法律渊源，更加贴近工程总承包管理的一线实务，努力呈现争议解决的“法律+造价+项目管理”综合思维，为工程总承包项目法律风险管理提出可资借鉴的意见和方案。

为了回应实务中常见的典型问题，本书亦设置了第三章“实务问答”专章，对实务人士提出的问题，依据现行的法律法规、政策性文件及裁判案例进行系统性的解答，并对项目管理提供法律意见和建议。

## P000011 (分10 84,799字 crosspool:EPC-CONTRACT unknown T1:0)
路径: 尼日利亚光伏EPC项目风险评估及对策研究——以S公司NASS光伏项目为例.pdf

 
  
 
 
 
 
 
 
Risk Assessment and 
Countermeasures for Photovoltaic EPC 
Projects in Nigeria: A Case Study of S 
Company's NASS Photovoltaic Project 
 尼日利亚光伏EPC 项目风险评估及对策研究——以S 公司NASS 光伏项目为例 
 
I
摘  要 
近年来，“碳中和”已经成为全球广泛接受的目标，全球80%以上的人口、
90%以上的经济体已经作出了碳中和承诺。随着“碳中和”行动的推进，光伏能
源基础设施建设需求和投资不断攀升，光伏基建工程的建设难度和成本不断提
高，采用工程总承包模式（Engineering, Procurement, and Construction，EPC）建设
光伏电站的风险越来越大，对光伏EPC 项目的风险管理进行研究，具有重要的理
论和实践意义。 
本文以尼日利亚地区光伏EPC 项目的风险为研究对象，应用风险管理理论，
得到了尼日利亚地区光伏EPC 项目风险管理的通用分析框架。框架运用PESTEL
工具、WBS 法和专家访谈法，从外部风险、内部风险维度详细分析了尼日利亚开
展光伏EPC 项目可能面临的9 大风险类型和28 个风险指标，本文还明确了尼日
利亚地区光伏EPC 项目风险评估的方法，提出降低风险的策略。该分析框架对在
尼日利亚建设的各类EPC 光伏项目的风险管理具有普遍适用性。 
在此基础上，本文以选择了S 公司在尼日利亚的NASS 光伏项目为案例，利
用前文建立的通用分析框架，对该项目工程建设过程中的所有潜在风险进行分
析。在风险识别方面，整理出NASS 光伏项目的风险清单，将NASS 光伏项目的
风险评估作为评估总目标，细分出了进度目标、质量目标、成本目标和安全目标
四个子目标；在风险评估过程中，本文邀请行业内不同领域的专家，结合风险要
素的发生概率和影响程度，对NASS 光伏项目不同风险要素两两比较打分，采用
层次分析法计算不同风险因素的风险水平。以NASS 光伏项目的实际情况对风险
评估框架进行推演，可以为其他企业开展类似项目提供借鉴和参考。 
关键词：光伏基建；EPC 项目风险；风险评估；风险管理；层次分析法 
 
 
 尼日利亚光伏EPC 项目风险评估及对策研究——以S 公司NASS 光伏项目为例 
 
II
Abstract 
In recent years, "carbon neutrality" has become a globally accepted goal, with over 
80% of the world's population and more than 90% of economies committing to achieving 
it. As the "carbon neutrality" initiative progresses, the demand and investment in 
photovoltaic (PV) energy infrastructure construction continue to rise, resulting in 
increasing difficulty and costs in PV infrastructure projects. The risk of adopting the 
Engineering, Procurement, and Construction (EPC) model for PV power plant 
construction has also grown, making the study of risk management in PV EPC projects 
crucial in both theoretical and practical aspects. 
This paper focuses on the risks of PV EPC projects in Nigeria, applying risk 
management theories to develop a general analytical framework for risk management in 
PV EPC projects in this region. The framework utilizes PESTEL analysis, Work 
Breakdown Structure (WBS) method, and expert interviews to comprehensively analyze 
nine major risk categories and 28 risk indicators that PV EPC projects in Nigeria may 
encounter, both from external and internal perspectives. The paper also clarifies the risk 
assessment methodology for PV EPC projects in Nigeria and proposes strategies to 
mitigate risks. This analytical framework has universal applicability for risk 
management in various EPC PV projects in Nigeria. 
Based on this framework, the paper selects the NASS PV project in Nigeria 
undertaken by Company S as a case study, analyzing all potential risks during the 
project's construction process. In terms of risk identification, a risk inventory for the 
NASS PV project is compiled, setting the overall risk assessment as the main objective 
and dividing it into four sub-objectives: schedule, quality, cost, and safety. In the risk 
assessment process, experts from different fields within the industry are invited to 
compare and score different risk factors based on their probability of occurrence and 
impact level, using the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) to calculate the risk levels of 
different factors. The application of this risk assessment framework to the actual 
 尼日利亚光伏EPC 项目风险评估及对策研究——以S 公司NASS 光伏项目为例 
 
III
situation of the NASS PV project provides valuable insights and references for other 
companies to undertake similar projects. 
KEY WORDS：Photovoltaic Infrastructure Construction；EPC Project Risks；Risk 
Assessment；Risk Management；Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP)

。与我一同工作的同志对本研究所做的任
何贡献均已在论文中作了明确的说明并表示了谢意

 中文摘要
中文摘要
随着业主要求和电力建设需求的增加

电力工程总承包的项目呈日渐增长之态势
。近凡年来

越来越多的电力设计
、施工企业都开始承担电力工程总承包项目
 它们不但在国内开展总承包业务

而且在国外也承担了大量的总承包项目
。特别是在电力紧缺的东南亚国家
 业主更乐意采取E PC
总
承包模式
。
由于在电力工程总承包项目中
 承包商需要承担极其复杂的各种风险
 且国内大多数电
力总承包企业规模不大
、实力有限
、经验欠缺
 往往很难胜任
 尽管有时也完成了总承包项目
 但
所得到的收益却少之又少
 或者遭受了较大的损失
。
本文在分析电力工程总承包的特点
、基本模式及范围
、风险识别过程的基础上
 研究了电力工
程总承包中主要存在的风险
 对电力工程总承包的风险评价与控制进行了论述
 并结合一个国内工
程总承包和三个国际工程总承包实例
 客观分析了工程中所存在的风险
。在国内的电力工程总承包
项目中
 政治(政策)风险是一个非常重要的因素
 特别是环保要求和拆迁政策对工程项目的实施起
着决定性的影响
; 而在国际工程实践中

自然条件风险
、施工方法风险和合同风险则是总承包商必
须认真对待的风险因素
。
通过本文的研究

旨在探讨在当前的政治环境
、市场环境和人文环境下

电力工程总承包商可
能会遇到的多种风险
 为总承包商进行风险管理积累一些经验
。
关键词
:
电力工程
; 工程总承包
; 风险

工程管理硕士（MEM）学位论文 
 
 
 
国际EPC 工程项目合同风险管理研究 
——以孟加拉国帕德玛大桥铁路项目为例 
Study on Contract Risk Management of International EPC 
Projects——The Padma Bridge Railway Project in Bangladesh 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
作 者 姓 名：        张春博          
研 究 方 向：       工程管理         
学       号：       22222168         
导 师 姓 名：        莫俊文          
完 成 日 期：       2025.5.23         
 
 
兰  州  交  通  大  学 
Lanzhou Jiaotong University 
                         兰州交通大学工程管理硕士（MEM）学位论文                         
I 
摘    要 
本文以孟加拉国帕德玛大桥铁路项目为研究对象，针对国际EPC 工程项目合同风
险的复杂性与动态性特征，系统构建覆盖施工方视角的风险管理框架。通过建立多维风
险框架，使用定性与定量相结合的风险识别方法，旨在解决跨国工程中风险识别模糊、
评估主观性及防控策略适配性不足的难题，为提升中国承包商在全球基建市场的风险韧
性提供方法论支撑。 
通过文献分析法筛选中国知网近三年、高引用文献20 篇，结合实地调研访谈（覆
盖11 个部门30 名专家），提取初始风险因素40 项，删除争议性指标5 项，最终形成包
含政治、商务、经济、环境、技术及管理6 个一级指标、35 个二级指标的风险评估体系。
研究提出了改进的AHP-TOPSIS 风险评估综合模型：在层次分析法（AHP）中引入区间
化语义约束概念，通过数学规划模型确定最优判断矩阵，降低主观偏差；在TOPSIS 模
型中优化正负理想解获取值，改进效益型与成本型指标的标准化公式，实现安全、质量、
进度、成本四大目标的独立评估与耦合计算。 
基于孟加拉国帕德玛大桥铁路项目（合同额31.39 亿美元，工期1643 天）的实证分
析表明，项目整体风险等级为2 级（较低风险），但关键风险呈现显著分化——气候水
文（权重0.072）与自然灾害（0.056）对进度目标影响显著，政局稳定（0.047）、政策连
续性（0.038）、通货膨胀（0.051）与汇率波动（0.040）对成本目标冲击突出。研究进一
步揭示风险要素与工程目标的映射关系：经济风险通过汇率波动与税收政策调整直接影
响成本控制，环境风险通过雨季停工与物流延误传导至进度目标，政治风险通过政策中
断与社会动荡系统威胁项目安全。基于此，提出“规避-转移-减轻-自留”防控策略：通
过政府协议锁定政策稳定性、优化宗教敏感期施工计划实现风险规避；采用主权担保信
用证与工程一切险转移金融与灾害风险；应用模块化施工技术与BIM 协同平台减轻气
候影响与设计变更；设立应急基金自留低概率可控风险。 
本研究的理论创新在于提出“多维风险因素分析框架”，改进的AHP-TOPSIS 模型
增强权重计算可信度，提升了标准值计算效率。实践层面，揭示了经济风险对成本目标
的影响、环境风险对进度目标的传导效应；构建“风险管控”协同机制，为“一带一路”
背景下国际EPC 工程合同风险管理提供了一定参考价值。 
 
关键词：国际EPC 工程；风险管理；层析分析法；优劣解距离法 
 
论文类型：应用研究 
 
 
            国际EPC 工程项目合同风险管理研究——以孟加拉国帕德玛大桥铁路项目为例                     
II 
Study on Contract Risk Management of International EPC Projects 
——The Padma Bridge Railway Project in Bangladesh 
Abstract 
This study focuses on the Padma Bridge Railway Project in Bangladesh to address the 
complexity and dynamic nature of contract risks in international EPC projects. It systematically 
constructs a comprehensive risk management framework from the contractor's perspective. By 
establishing a multidimensional risk framework and employing a combination of qualitative 
and quantitative risk identification methods, the study aims to address the challenges of 
ambiguous risk identification, subjective risk assessment, and insufficient adaptability of 
prevention strategies in cross-border engineering projects. This research provides 
methodological support for enhancing Chinese contractors' risk resilience in the global 
infrastructure market. 
A risk assessment system was developed through literature analysis (prioritizing high-
impact publications) and field research (including expert interviews). Initial risk factors were 
refined via the Delphi method, resulting in a hierarchical structure comprising six primary 
indicators (political, commercial, economic, environmental, technical, and managerial risks) 
and 35 secondary indicators. An enhanced AHP-TOPSIS model was proposed: interval-based 
semantic constraints were integrated into the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) to optimize 
judgment matrices and reduce subjectivity, while the TOPSIS model was refined by redefining 
ideal solutions and improving normalization formulas for benefit- and cost-type indicators, 
enabling independent evaluation and integrated analysis of safety, quality, schedule, and cost 
objectives. 
Empirical analysis of the Padma Bridge Railway Project revealed an overall risk level of 
Grade 2, with critical risks exhibiting distinct impacts. Environmental risks  significantly 
affected schedule objectives, while political risks and economic risks disproportionately 
threatened safety and cost targets. The study further identified risk-objective linkages: 
economic risks directly influenced cost control through fiscal policy shifts, environmental risks 
disrupted schedules via seasonal delays, and political risks systematically jeopardized project 
safety through socio-political instability. A four-tiered risk mitigation strategy ("avoidance-
transfer-mitigation-retention") was formulated, emphasizing policy stabilization, financial risk 
transfer through sovereign guarantees, technical adaptations to environmental challenges, and 
contingency reserves for low-probability risks. 
Theoretically, this research introduces a multidimensional risk analysis framework and 
enhances the credibility of weight calculations through the improved AHP-TOPSIS model. 
Practically, it elucidates the cascading effects of economic and environmental risks on project 
objectives and establishes a synergistic risk governance mechanism. These contributions 
provide actionable insights for managing contract risks in international EPC projects under the 
                         兰州交通大学工程管理硕士（MEM）学位论文                         
III 
Belt and Road Initiative, balancing theoretical rigor with operational feasibility. 
 
KEY WORDS：International EPC Projects，Risk Management，AHP，TOPSIS

浙江大学硕士学位论文                                                                    摘要 
II 
摘要 
核电工程一般采用EPC总承包固定总价合同，总承包商承担设计、采购、施
工的全部责任和风险，履行进度、成本、质量、HSE、合同等要素管理职责。核
电EPC工程建设阶段多要素管理离散，缺乏统一价值衡量准则和管理机制，导致
资源管理分散和浪费、重点任务清单失真、交付成果价值度不高等系列问题。 
本文在充分汲取国内外文献资料理论成果基础上，根据核电EPC工程管理特
征和优势，以实现多要素协同管理和统一管理价值评判准则为目的，设计“要素
-计划-实施”三维结构模型，重点研究模型构建，主要内容如下： 
1）要素管理为知识维，主要研究要素管理关键因素和要素价值评判准则。
利用第一性原理，提出要素特征属性概念，以特征属性作为要素管理活动的价值
标签。运用调查问卷法、专家经验法和SPSS26.0统计分析，形成要素特征属性集、
多要素管理价值指标集，涉及进度、成本、质量、HSE、技术等17类要素、51
项要素特征属性，要素评判准则分三个维度：目标要素、能力建设、干系人管理，
共包括18项一级指标和76项二级指标。 
2）计划管理为时间维，构建三层级计划架构：核电工程建设四阶段-EPC一
体化计划-要素管理计划。本文重点研究工程施工建造阶段的多要素综合计划，
包括EPC一体化计划和要素管理计划。分析综合计划架构、内在关联链路，实现
综合计划的时限、逻辑、关联管理。通过工程管理特征和管理要素的关联分析，
梳理出常用的要素管理计划，其要素管理活动是价值评价和决策对象。 
3）多属性决策管理方法实施过程为主逻辑维，用于赋予要素管理活动价值，
对管理活动作出价值评估和决策。赋值涉及两个维度：要素特征属性和价值评判
准则。本文构建两个维度决策矩阵，分析要素管理活动与决策矩阵对应关系，运
用三角模糊TOPSIS多属性决策管理方法和主、客观赋权法，对要素管理活动作
价值评估和决策管理，根据决策结果评价管理机制效用。 
本文研究核电EPC工程多要素管理三维模型及拓扑关系，提出一种多要素管
理价值量化和决策机制，以核电EPC工程S项目为实例，选取48个典型要素管理
活动为价值评估对象，将管理机制应用于工程。 
关键词：核电EPC工程；霍尔三维模型；多要素管理；TOPSIS多属性决策
方法. 
  
浙江大学硕士学位论文                                                           ABSTRACT 
III 
 
Abstract 
Projects of nuclear power engineering are used to adopting EPC (design, 
procurement, construction) general contracting model with fixed-total-cost price 
contracts. EPC general contractors almost undertake all the responsibilities and risks 
of design, procurement, construction, and perform management for multi-factors, 
such as schedule, cost, quality, HSE, and contract etc. In the construction stage of 
nuclear power EPC engineering, the discretization of factor management ,lacking 
unified value measurement criteria and management mechanism, leads to a series of 
problems such as scattered and wasted resource management, distorted key work list, 
and low value of delivered results. 
Based on the theoretical achievements of domestic and foreign literature, and 
according to the management characteristics and advantages of nuclear power EPC 
engineering, 
this 
paper 
designs 
a 
three-dimensional 
structure 
model 
of 
"factor-plan-implementation" for the purpose of realizing multi-factor collaborative 
management and unified value evaluation criteria. The model building is the main 
research contents as follows:  
1) Factor management is a knowledge dimension, mainly studying the key factors 
and multi-factor management value evaluation criteria. By using the first principle, 
the concept is proposed about factor characteristic attribute as the value label of factor 
management activities. By using questionnaire method, expert experience method and 
SPSS26.0 statistical analysis, the factor characteristic attribute set and multi-factor 
management value index set are formed, involving 17 categories of factors, 51 factor 
characteristic attributes, such as progress, cost, quality, HSE, technology. The 
evaluation criteria of factors are divided into three dimensions: target factors, capacity 
building, stakeholder management, including 18 first-level and 76 second-level 
indicators.  
  
浙江大学硕士学位论文                                                           ABSTRACT 
IV 
 
2) The plan management is the time dimension, and a three-level plan structure is 
constructed: four stages of nuclear power engineering construction - EPC integration 
plan - factor management plan. The multi-factor comprehensive plan in the 
construction stage of the project is concerned , including EPC integration plan and 
factor management plan. The plan structure and internal link are analyzed to realize 
the time limit and logical management. Through the analysis of the relationship 
between engineering characteristics and factor management, the common factor 
management plan is sorted out, and the factor activities are the objects of value 
evaluation and decision-making. 
 3) The implementation process of multi-attribute decision-making management 
method is the main logical dimension, which is used to give value to the factor 
activities through making value evaluation and decision-making. The assignment 
involves two dimensions: factor characteristic attribute and value evaluation criteria . 
By construction of two-dimensional decision matrix, and the analysis of the 
corresponding relationship between factor activities and decision matrix, triangular 
fuzzy TOPSIS multi-attribute decision-making management (MADM) method and 
subjective and objective weighting method is used for the value evaluation and 
decision-making management of factor activities. According to the decision results , 
management utility is evaluated.  
This paper establishes a multi-factor management value quantification and 
decision-making mechanism about nuclear power EPC engineering through studing 
model three dimensionals and their topological relationship. Taking S project of 
nuclear power engineering as an example, 48 typical factor activities are selected as 
the value evaluation objects for the apllication. 
 Key words: Nuclear power EPC engineering; Hall 3-D model; Multi-factor 
management ; MADM method based on TOPSIS.

PCA-TOPSIS 模型在铁路工程EPC 项目风险评估中的应用 
黄志婷 
  
 
A Master’s Thesis in Industrial Engineering and Management 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
Candidate:  Huang Zhiting 
Supervisor：Professor Dong Hai 
 
 
 
 
School of Mechanical Engineering 
Shenyang University 
June 9th, 2023 
The Application of PCA-TOPSIS Model in Risk Evaluation 
of EPC Railway Project 
  
I 
摘要 
2014 年，中国开始启用EPC（工程，采购和施工）的工程模式，随着国家对开
展工程总承包业务相关政策的不断完善，我国部分先进企业“十三五”期间已经纷
纷顺应工程总承包发展趋势，在设计管理方面积极开展探索和实践、加强设计资源
的整合；2021 年是“十四五”开局之年，随着工程总承包的推行力度逐渐加大，国
家对建筑企业打通全产业链提出更高要求。EPC 工程总承包模式自三十多年前引进
发展以来，在中国取得了显著的增长和发展。如今，这种模式得到了政府各部委的
广泛认可，用于大型建筑项目。随着EPC 铁路工程项目应用广泛 ，EPC 相对传统承
包DB 模式风险因素发生概率和造成的损失大比例上升，因此，EPC 风险管理是工
程项目风险管理的核心。针对EPC 工程项目管理中普遍存在的项目工期长、投资金
额巨大、项目管理和协调难度大等一系列问题，本文提出如下研究内容：  
首先，通过文献研究法和德尔菲法全面整理识别铁路EPC 项目工程外部环境因
素和分析，研究铁路工程EPC 模式项目指标风险之间的关系，建立铁路工程EPC
总承包模式风险评估框架图；分别研究政治风险、市场风险、社会风险、法律风
险、自然风险等五类风险，建立EPC 铁路工程项目专门的风险评价一级指标体系和
二级指标体系。 
其次，基于熵权法建立PCA-TOPSIS 风险评估预测模型。其中，对熵权分析法
和TOPSIS 方法的优缺点和适用性等问题进行了介绍和分析，确定主体客观权重，
利用最小熵原理进行组合优化赋权，构建铁路工程EPC 项目风险评估模型。在理论
分析的基础上建立熵权TOPSIS 法和PCA 法相结合的风险评估模型，用于对铁路工
程EPC 项目风险进行评估。 
最后，通过引入中泰铁路数据进行实证研究，对海外中泰铁路工程EPC 项目风
险进行评估。结果表明，建立的PCA-TOPSIS 模型，对于本研究中的EPC 工程铁路
项目风险研究是合理和实用的，本文对于铁路工程EPC 建设项目风险评估模型的研
 沈阳大学硕士学位论文 
II 
究具有理论和现实意义，可以对铁路建设项目的建设风险进行有效的评估。降低评
价过程中对不确定因素处理的复杂性，提高传统TOPSIS 评价函数的处理效率，规
避了传统TOPSIS 模型人为选取主要影响因子的干扰。 
 
关键词：铁路工程， PCA-TOPSIS，风险评估，预测 
 
 
 
 
 
  
III 
The Application of PCA-TOPSIS Model in Risk Evaluation of 
EPC Railway Project 
Abstract 
Since its introduction more than 30 years ago, the general contracting model of EPC 
Engineering has achieved remarkable growth and development in China. Today, this 
model is widely accepted by government ministries and departments for use in large-scale 
construction. With the extensive application of EPC railway project, EPC risk management 
is the core of project risk management because of its higher probability of occurrence and 
loss ratio than traditional contractual DB model. Aiming at a series of problems in EPC 
engineering project management, such as long project duration, large investment amount 
and difficulty in project management and coordination, the following research contents are 
put forward: 
Firstly, through literature research method and Delphi method, the external 
environment factors and analysis of railway EPC project are comprehensively sorted out, 
the relationship between the index risk of railway EPC project is studied, and the risk 
assessment framework of railway EPC project is established. Political risk, market risk, 
social risk, legal risk and natural risk are studied respectively, and a special risk evaluation 
index system for EPC railway project is set up.   
Secondly, the PCA-TOPSIS risk assessment prediction model is established based on 
entropy weight method. The paper introduces and analyzes the advantages and 
disadvantages of entropy weight analysis and TOPSIS method, determines the objective 
weight of the subject, uses the principle of minimum entropy for combination optimization, 
and constructs the risk assessment model of EPC project in railway engineering. On the 
basis of theoretical analysis, a risk assessment model combining entropy weighted TOPSIS 
method and PCA method is established to evaluate the risk of railway engineering EPC 
project.  
Finally, through the introduction of Sino-Thai railway data for empirical research, the 
risk assessment of the overseas Sino-Thai railway project EPC project. The results show 
that the PCA-TOPSIS model is reasonable and practical for the risk study of EPC 
engineering railway project in this study. It reduces the complexity of uncertainty 
 沈阳大学硕士学位论文 
IV 
processing, improves the efficiency of traditional TOPSIS evaluation function, and avoids 
the interference of the traditional TOPSIS model. Secondly, a PCA-TOPSIS risk 
assessment prediction model is established based on the entropy weight method. Among 
them, the advantages, disadvantages and applicability of the entropy weight analysis 
method and the TOPSIS method are introduced and analyzed, the objective weight of the 
subject is determined, the combination optimization and empowerment is carried out by 
using the minimum entropy principle, and the risk assessment model of railway 
engineering EPC project is constructed. On the basis of theoretical analysis, a risk 
assessment model combining the entropy weight TOPSIS method and the PCA method 
was established for the risk assessment of railway engineering EPC projects.  
 
Key words: railway construction；risk evaluation；prediction；PCA-TOPSIS metho

 南京航空航天大学硕士学位论文 
I 
摘  要 
目前，随着国家大力推进工程总承包模式，工程总承包（EPC）模式在我国正逐步成为我
国建筑行业的主流引领模式。由于国内的施工总承包单位对工程总承包（EPC）管理模式认识
不足，管理水平与国际一流管理水平相比有着相当大的差距，造成了很多工程总承包项目承包
单位亏损或业主单位在合约规定的工期内无法得到满意的交付成果。建筑工程项目实施进程中
施工承包商管理能力的不成熟直接影响项目的工期、质量，最终导致项目成本管控混乱，从而
产生的工期延长、质量降低都大大增加了建设业主单位投资控制风险和交付风险。因此，在建
设业主单位无法完全放手EPC 项目投资控制的风险管理的当下，主动地参与项目全过程的投
资控制相关风险管理就是十分必要的。 
本文以工程总承包项目实施的各阶段顺序为风险分析的主线，以工程投资控制的相关风险
为风险识别要素，结合K 公司已建成的工程总承包项目实例为研究样本，研究建设方业主单
位工程总承包项目投资控制的风险管理体系。通过收集K 公司已完成工程总承包项目的相关
文件进行整理、归纳后，运用检查表法、问卷调查法通过专家进行风险识别，对风险因素发生
的概率和影响大小进行比较选择评价，使用层次分析法对评价结果进行赋值和排序，从而从定
量分析角度得出各个风险影响程度等级。通过对项目风险主要影响因素的总结，结合项目经验，
提出合理的应对措施，并提出部分应对方法在在建项目中实施的效果。降低工程总承包建设业
主单位的项目投资控制风险，提高项目风险管控能力，为其他建设业主单位的工程总承包项目
投资控制风险管理工作提供参考借鉴。 
 
关键词：工程总承包，投资控制，风险管理，检查表法，层次分析法 
 K 公司EPC 项目投资控制的风险管理研究 
II                                                                                               
ABSTRACT 
At present, as the country vigorously promotes the EPC mode, the EPC mode is gradually 
becoming the mainstream leading mode in my country's construction industry. Due to the lack of 
understanding of the EPC management mode by domestic construction general contractors, there is a 
considerable gap between the management level and the international first-class management level, 
resulting in many EPC project contracting units losing money or the owner unit being unable to obtain 
satisfactory results within the construction period stipulated in the contract. Deliver results. During the 
implementation of construction projects, the immature management capabilities of construction 
contractors directly affect the project duration and quality, ultimately leading to confusion in project 
cost control. The resulting extension of the duration and lower quality greatly increase the investment 
control risks and delivery risks of the construction owner. . Therefore, at a time when the construction 
owner cannot completely let go of the risk management of EPC project investment control, it is very 
necessary to actively participate in the investment control-related risk management of the entire 
project process. 
This article takes the sequence of each stage of EPC project implementation as the main line of 
risk analysis, takes the risks related to engineering investment control as risk identification elements, 
and combines the completed EPC project examples of K Company as a research sample to study the 
construction owner's EPC project investment control Risk management system. After collecting and 
sorting out the relevant documents of the EPC project that Company K has completed, experts use the 
checklist method and questionnaire survey method to identify risks, compare and select the evaluation 
of the probability and impact of risk factors, and use the analytic hierarchy process to The evaluation 
results are assigned and sorted to obtain each risk impact level from a quantitative analysis 
perspective. By summarizing the main influencing factors of project risks and combining project 
experience, reasonable countermeasures are proposed, and the effects of some countermeasures 
implemented in projects under construction are proposed. Reduce project investment risks of EPC 
construction owners, improve project risk management and control capabilities, and provide reference 
for other construction owners’ EPC project investment risk management work. 
 
Key words: engineering procurement construction, investment control, risk management, checklist 
method, analytic hierarchy process

总承包商视角下工程总承包总价 
合同风险分析及其风险应对研究 
 
（申请硕士学位） 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
专业学位类别： 工程硕士 
专业(领域)：工业工程 
研究方向：质量管理与可靠性工程

2022 年2 月 
 - 2 - 
 
Thesis Submitted to Tianjin University of Technology 
for the Master’s Degree 
 
      
 
  
Research on risk analysis and risk 
response of Lump Sum Contract 
from the perspective of general 
contractor 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
By 
ZHOU jinchao 
 
 
Supervisor 
Prof. Yan Ling 
 
 
 
Fer.2022 
 摘要 
自2016 年住建部发布12 号文颁布以来，国内工程总承包市场的热度陡然上
涨，市场竞争激烈，总承包商企业规模逐渐壮大，但仍存在盈利能力不足、利润
率低迷等生存问题。鉴于业主选择实施工程总承包模式时，不同的合同计价模式
会导致总承包商面临风险程度以及利润空间各异。为应对日益复杂的项目管理风
险，总承包商亟需良好的合同风险管理能力，并且研究表明，适当合理的进行风
险防范，有助于总承包商的创收能力提升。为保证新发展阶段下总承包商鲜活生
命力，有必要深入探究工程总承包合同风险因素，因此，本文拟从总承包商角度，
对工程总承包总价合同风险进行以下研究：  
1.引入质量管理体系中的亲和图法对工程总承包合同风险因素进行识别。通
过文献梳理法、案例分析法和线上访谈法等质性研究方法，分析出项目中存在的
触发风险的事件，将可能对总承包商产生影响并承担后果的风险事件绘制成总承
包总价合同风险事件调查表，然后通过头脑风暴绘制亲和图，对工程总承包模式
下总承包商所承担的合同风险事件进行归类汇总：工程范围风险、发包人要求风
险、合同总价风险、合同条件风险、设计管理风险、现场条件风险、审计风险、
总承包商内部管理风险。 
2.基于投资偏差的工程总承包合同状态分析。基于合同状态理论，从投资目
标差异的方向构建工程总承包总价合同状态分析概念模型。从产生的风险传递链
条的设计、施工、竣工三个阶段出发，采用多案例研究方法从海量案例中甄别出
针对每个阶段的独特风险事件，然后将投资目标造成的工程量和合同价格的偏差
影响作为风险后果。研究结果表明设计阶段风险偏向于合同条件风险、发包人要
求风险、设计管理风险以及工程范围风险；施工阶段偏向于现场条件风险、总承
包商内部管理风险、合同总价风险；竣工阶段偏向于审计风险。 
3.基于PDPC 的工程总承包合同风险防范与创收策略。基于三阶段因事件源
引起的合同风险分类，采用顺向思维的PDPC 法，提出引起工程变更的工程范围、
发包人要求、设计管理、现场条件风险防范；引起调整价格的合同总价、合同条
件、总承包商内部管理的风险防范以及引起索赔的审计风险防范，提出总承包商
的创收策略。 
综上所述，运用亲和图通过工程总承包总价合同风险因素的辨识，然后运用
多案例分析总承包项目全过程中合同状态变化差异，在此基础上结合计价模式，
运用PDPC 法有针对性的提出不同类型风险防范与创收策略，对拓展总承包商创
收思路具有实践价值。 
 
关键词：工程总承包, 总价合同, 合同风险, 风险防范 
 2 
 
Abstract 
 
Since the issuance of Document No. 12 by the Ministry of Housing and Urban-
Rural Development in 2016, the popularity of the domestic general project contracting 
market has risen sharply, the market competition has been fierce, and the scale of 
general contractors has gradually grown, but there are still survival problems such as 
insufficient profitability and low profit margins. In view of the fact that when the owner 
chooses to implement the general contracting model of the project, different contract 
pricing models will lead to different levels of risk and profit margins for the general 
contractor. In order to cope with the increasingly complex project management risks, 
general contractors urgently need good contract risk management capabilities, and 
studies have shown that proper and reasonable risk prevention will help the general 
contractors to improve their ability to generate revenue. In order to ensure the fresh 
vitality of the general contractor in the new development stage, it is necessary to deeply 
explore the risk factors of the general contract of the project. Therefore, this paper 
intends to conduct the following research on the risk of the general contract of the 
general contract from the perspective of the general contractor: 
1. Introduce the affinity diagram method in the quality management system to 
identify the risk factors of the general contracting contract. Through qualitative research 
methods such as literature review method, case analysis method and online interview 
method, the events that trigger risks in the project are analyzed, and the risk events that 
may affect the general contractor and bear the consequences are drawn into the total 
contract price Contract risk event survey form, and then draw an affinity diagram 
through brainstorming to classify and summarize the contract risk events undertaken by 
the general contractor under the general project contracting mode: project scope risk, 
contractor requirement risk, total contract price risk, contract risk Condition risk, design 
management risk, site condition risk, audit risk, general contractor internal management 
risk. 
2. Status analysis of general contracting contract based on investment deviation. 
Based on the contract state theory, a conceptual model of the state analysis of the 
general contracting price contract is constructed from the direction of the difference of 
investment objectives. Starting from the three stages of design, construction and 
completion of the resulting risk transfer chain, the multi-case study method is used to 
identify the unique risk events for each stage from a large number of cases, and then 
 the deviation of the amount of work and the contract price caused by the investment 
target is calculated. Impact as a consequence of risk. The research results show that the 
risk in the design stage is biased towards the risk of contract conditions, the risk of the 
developer's requirements, the risk of design management and the risk of the project 
scope; the risk in the construction stage is biased towards the risk of site conditions, the 
internal management risk of the general contractor and the risk of the total contract 
price; the stage of completion is biased towards auditing risk. 
3. Risk prevention and revenue-generating strategy of general contracting contract 
based on PDPC. Based on the three-stage classification of contract risks caused by event 
sources, the PDPC method of forward thinking is used to propose the project scope, 
contractor requirements, design management, and site condition risk prevention that 
cause engineering changes; the total contract price and contract conditions that cause 
price adjustments , the risk prevention of the general contractor's internal management 
and the audit risk prevention of claims, and put forward the general contractor's 
revenue-generating strategy. 
To sum up, use the affinity diagram to identify the risk factors of the general 
contracting price contract of the project, and then use multiple cases to analyze the 
difference of the contract status in the whole process of the general contracting project. 
On this basis, combined with the pricing model, the PDPC method is used to target 
Different types of risk preventi

May, 2023
 工程总承包联合体内部合同风险管理研究
II
摘
要
根据国民经济统计公报，自2009 年以来，建筑业产量总产值始终保持上升态
势，产量增加值占国内生产总值的比例稳定保持在6.5%以上。建筑业在国民经济
支柱产业的地位稳固，发展态势良好，工程总承包模式作为建筑业最常使用的施
工管理模式，高效地促成了许多大中型基础建筑项目的建造与使用。2020 年3 月
1 日《房屋建筑和市政基础设施项目工程总承包管理办法》对工程总承包模式设
计（施工）单位的联合体组成提出更明确的资质要求，标志着工程总承包模式正
式步入“设计+施工”的双资质时代。一些暂不具备“设计+施工”双资质的设计
（施工）企业，可以通过组成联合体后进行工程总承包。可以预见，今后采用联
合体模式承接工程总承包项目的情形会屡见不鲜。但由于目前国内工程总承包联
合体模式相关立法仍在完善过程中，近年联合体项目的合同纠纷问题又层出不穷，
因此本文针对工程总承包模式承包方联合体间的合同风险管理问题进行了下列研
究：
首先，分析了国内外工程总承包模式下联合体的组成、管理情况及相关研究
现状，梳理工程总承包联合体、合同管理、风险管理的概念及内涵；整理了直觉
三角模糊数、多属性群决策方法相关研究理论；介绍了法律逻辑及法律论证过程
等法学知识。
其次，围绕合同风险清单建立及风险因素属性评价构建工程总承包联合体模
式合同风险评价体系。通过法律论证模式整理了2018 年至今因不同原因产生法律
纠纷的工程总承包联合体模式裁判文书，结合相关工程领域、法学领域专家实践
经验，厘清了现存工程总承包联合体模式的合同风险清单；结合联合体模式现状
确定了风险属性，设计了相关统计问卷。
再次，构建工程总承包联合体模式合同风险评价模型。将问卷信息转化为直
觉三角模糊数，通过矩阵计算，先后进行基于专家权重、属性权重的两次几何算
子集结，运用TOPSIS 排序法将集结结果进行排序，达到风险评估的目的。
最后，引入采用工程总承包联合体模式的M 项目作为本文的研究依托。介绍
M 项目的基本概况及联合体各成员的基本情况，运用合同风险评价模型进行分析，
验证模型的有效性及实用性，并提出相应的合同风险规避建议。研究表明，本文
建立的风险评价模型能规避联合体成立带来的合同风险，对联合体协议签订具有
一定的指导作用。
关键词：工程总承包；承包方；联合体；直觉三角模糊数；合同风险管理
 工程硕士论文
III
Abstract
According to the National Economic Statistical Bulletin, the total output value of
the construction industry has been on the rise since 2009, and the ratio of output
added value to the GDP has remained stable at above 6.5%. The construction industry
has a stable position in the pillar industry of the national economy, and the
development trend is good. As the most commonly used construction management
mode in the construction industry, the general contract mode has effectively
contributed to the construction and use of many large and medium-sized basic
construction projects. On March 1, 2020, the General Contracting Management
Measures for Housing Construction and Municipal Infrastructure Projects put forward
more explicit qualification requirements for the consortium composition of the design
(construction) units of the general contracting mode of the project, marking that the
general contracting mode of the project has officially entered the double qualification
era of "design & construction". Some design (construction) enterprises that do not
have the dual qualification of "design & construction" can form a consortium to carry
out the general contract of the project. It can be predicted that in the future, the use of
consortium model to undertake the general contract project situation will be common.
However,
due
to
the
current
domestic
legislation
of
the
general
contracting
consortium mode is still in the process of improvement, and the contract disputes of
the consortium projects in recent years have emerged in an endless stream, this paper
has conducted the following research on the contract risk management of the
contractor consortium in the general contracting mode:
Firstly, it analyzes the composition, management and related research status of
the consortium under the general project contracting mode at home and abroad, and
sorts out the concept and connotation of the general project contracting consortium,
contract management and risk management. The research theories of intuitionistic
triangular fuzzy number and multi-attribute group decision making methods are
summarized. Legal logic and legal argumentation process are introduced.
Secondly, the contract risk evaluation system of general contract consortium
model is built around the establishment of contract risk list and risk factor attribute
evaluation. Through the legal demonstration mode, the judgment documents of the
general project contracting consortium mode caused by different reasons from 2018 to
now are sorted out. Combined with the practical experience of experts in related
 工程总承包联合体内部合同风险管理研究
IV
engineering fields and legal fields, the contract risk list of the existing general project
contracting consortium mode is clarified. Combined with the status quo of association
mode, the risk attribute was determined and the relevant statistical questionnaire was
designed.
Thirdly, the risk evaluation model of general contract consortium is constructed.
The questionnaire information was transformed into an intuitive triangular fuzzy
number. Through matrix calculation, two geometric operators were aggregated based
on expert weight and attribute weight, and the aggregated results were sorted by
TOPSIS ranking method to achieve the purpose of risk assessment.
Finally, M project, which adopts the mode of general contracting consortium, is
introduced as the basis of this paper. This paper introduces the general situation of M
project and the basic situation of each member of the consortium, uses the contract
risk assessment model for analysis, verifies the effectiveness and practicability of the
model, and puts forward the corresponding contract risk avoidance suggestions. The
research shows that the risk assessment model established in this paper can avoid the
contract risk caused by the establishment of joint entity, and has a certain guiding role
in the signing of joint agreement.
Key words: Project general contracting; The contractor; Consortium; Intuitive
triangle fuzzy number; Contract risk management

 摘 要 
 I 
S设计公司EPC工程总承包管理能力评价研究 
学位类型：专业学位 
学科专业：工程管理（MEM） 
摘 要 
随着我国国民经济的高质量发展，建筑行业固定资产投资比重在缓慢下降。设计
企业的市场经营压力日益增加，近年来迅速发展的EPC 工程总承包业务为设计企业
持续健康发展提供了机会。S 设计公司为一家以工程设计、监理为主要业务的综合性
工程服务公司，在设计和监理业务持续下滑的市场背景下，正在大力拓展EPC 工程
总承包业务，但是目前S 设计公司的EPC 工程总承包管理能力无法满足项目管理的
需要。因此，通过科学客观的评价研究，找出影响S 设计公司EPC 总承包管理能力
的关键因素并提出有针对性的改进对策，具有重要的现实意义。 
本文主要研究过程如下：首先，介绍研究背景并提出本文的研究内容、方法和技
术路线。其次，对相关概念和理论进行阐述，为本文研究奠定理论基础。然后，介绍
S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理现状，采用问卷调查法找出S 设计公司EPC 工程总
承包管理存在的主要问题。接着，通过文献研究和问卷调查法对影响设计企业EPC 工
程总承包管理能力的关键因素进行识别和筛选，进而构建S 设计公司EPC 工程总承
包管理能力评价指标体系，并采用组合赋权法计算评价指标体系权重。随后，建立模
糊综合评价模型，对S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能力进行评价，并对评价结果
进行分析找出影响S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能力的关键因素。最后，根据评
价指标体系权重和评价结果提出改进S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能力的对策。 
本文通过构建评价指标体系和评价模型，对S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能
力进行了模糊综合评价，找出了影响S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能力的关键因
素并提出了改进对策。研究结果为提升S 设计公司EPC 工程总承包管理能力奠定了
理论基础，也为同类设计企业提升EPC 工程总承包管理能力提供了一定的借鉴。 
关键词：设计公司；EPC 工程总承包；管理能力；模糊综合评价 
论文类型：应用研究
 ABSTRACT 
III 
 
Research on Evaluation of EPC Engineering General 
Contracting Management Capability of S Design Institute 
Type of Degree： Professional degree 
Specialty： Engineering Management 
ABSTRACT 
With the high-quality development of China's national economy, the proportion of fixed 
assets investment in the construction industry is slowly declining. Design enterprises are 
facing significant market survival pressure, and the rapidly developing EPC engineering 
general contracting business in recent years has provided opportunities for the sustainable 
and healthy development of design enterprises. S Company is a comprehensive engineering 
service company with engineering design and supervision as its main business. Against the 
backdrop of a continuous decline in design business, it is actively transitioning to EPC 
engineering general contracting business. However, the company's management capabilities 
cannot meet the needs of EPC engineering general contracting projects, which seriously 
affects the development of EPC engineering general contracting business. Therefore, it is 
necessary to identify the key factors that affect S Company's EPC general contracting 
management ability through quantitative research and develop targeted measures to enhance 
the EPC engineering general contracting management ability. 
The main research process of this article is as follows: Firstly, introduce the research 
background and propose the research content, methods, and technical roadmap of this article. 
Secondly, elaborate on relevant concepts and theories to lay the theoretical foundation for 
this study. Then, introduce the current situation of EPC engineering general contracting 
management in S Design Company, and use questionnaire survey method to identify the 
main problems in EPC engineering general contracting management in S Design Company. 
Subsequently, through literature research and questionnaire survey methods, key factors 
affecting the EPC engineering general contracting management capability of design 
enterprises were identified and screened, and an evaluation index system for the EPC 
 西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文 
IV 
 
engineering general contracting management capability of S Design Company was 
constructed. The weight of the evaluation index system was calculated using the combination 
weighting method. Subsequently, a fuzzy comprehensive evaluation model was established 
to evaluate the EPC engineering general contracting management capability of S Design 
Company, and the evaluation results were analyzed to identify the key factors affecting the 
EPC engineering general contracting management capability of S Design Company. Finally, 
based on the weight of the evaluation index system and the evaluation results, propose 
measures to improve the EPC engineering general contracting management capability of S 
Design Company. 
This article constructs an evaluation index system and evaluation model to conduct a 
fuzzy comprehensive evaluation of S Design Company's EPC engineering general 
contracting management ability, identifies key issues that affect S Design Company's EPC 
engineering general contracting management ability, and proposes improvement measures. 
The research results have laid the foundation for improving the EPC engineering general 
contracting management capability of S Design Company, and also have certain reference 
significance for evaluating the EPC engineering general contracting management capability 
of similar design enterprises. 
 
Keywords: Design Institute；EPC engineering general contracting；Management 
capability；Fuzzy comprehensive evaluation 
 
Paper Type: Applied Research

## P000020 (分9 86,701字 crosspool:EPC-CONTRACT media T1:0)

 南京航空航天大学硕士学位论文
I
摘
要
EPC 总承包模式是近年来在国际上流行的一种项目管理模式，也是当前工程项目建设中被
采用次数最多的模式之一。在EPC 总承包模式在中国飞速发展的背景下，设计单位也开始在总
承包市场上以EPC 模式参与工程竞标，但与传统工程公司相比，设计院不熟悉项目施工与项目
管理，缺乏具有项目管理经验的项目管理人才，不具备优势，在总承包市场上面临一系列风险。
因此，研究以设计院为总承包商的EPC 项目的风险管理具有一定的理论意义和实践价值。
本文以H 设计集团为例，收集了以H 设计集团为总承包商的EPC 项目资料，并对集团内
参与过EPC 项目的6 名员工进行访谈，采用基于扎根理论的三级编码对收集到的文本资料进行
分析，得到EPC 项目的10 个一级风险因素及36 个二级风险因素。以H 设计集团为总承包商
的武义PK 竞艺会展综合体项目为案例，以三级编码分析获得的风险因素为指标体系，采用层
次分析法确定项目的指标权重。分析发现，经济风险权重最高，其次是工程协调风险、政策与
合约风险以及施工风险。在二级风险指标中，分包履约风险、费用控制风险、施工安全风险、
外部沟通风险及业主支付风险较高。结合求得的风险指标权重，运用模糊综合评价法对EPC 项
目风险进行定量分析研究，得到该项目整体的风险水平等级为高风险。
基于本文的研究发现，笔者提出了基于风险权重-等级的四分图，针对不同象限的风险因素
分别提出了对策建议，指导风险管理部门采取相应的风险应对措施。
关键词：EPC 项目，层次分析法，扎根理论，模糊综合评价法，风险管理
 H 设计集团EPC 项目风险管理研究
II
ABSTRACT
EPC general contracting model is one of the most popular project management mode in
international. It is also one of the most adopted mode in project construction currently. With the fast
development of EPC general contracting model, the design institutions are gradually participating in
the project bidding in the general contracting market. However, compared to traditional engineering
company, the design institutions are not familiar with construction and project management. They also
lack managerial resources that familiar with project management. With these disadvantages, they face
a list of risks in the general contracting market. Therefore, to investigate the risk management of EPC
project with design institute as the general contractor can provide both theoretical contributions and
practical implications.
This study use the H design institute as the case to study the EPC project risk management. The
documents about the EPC project with H design institute as the general contractor are collected. Six
employees in H design institute who have participated in EPC project are interviewed. Based on
project and interview transcript, the three-level coding analysis based on ground theory was
conducted. The results show that there are 36 second level risk factors which belong to 10 first level
risk factors. The EPC project of Wuyi PK complex was used as a specific case and AHP method was
adopted to analyze the weights of the risk factors identified. The results show that economic factor is
most important, followed by coordination risk, policy and contract risk, and construction safety risk.
In the second level factors, subcontract implementation risk, fees control risk, construction safety risk,
and external coordination risk are more important than other risks. Combined with the weights we
obtained, the fuzzy comprehensive evaluation method was used to quantitatively analyze the risk level
of the EPC project. The results show that the Wuyi EPC project has high level risk.
Based on the data analysis results and findings, this study proposed a few suggestions to cope
with the subcontract implementation risk. These suggestions are expected to guide the design
institutions to make policies to minimize the risks when undertaking EPC projects.
Keywords:
EPC project, AHP method, ground theory, fuzzy comprehensive evaluation method, risk
management

 华北电力大学硕士学位论文 
I 
 
摘    要 
随着市场经济体制的不断加深，电力设计院大力发展总承包的业务模式，发挥设计
院以设计龙头的优势，发挥对设备采购、工程建设的引领作用。但目前电力设计院仍处
于EPC 项目粗放式管理的模式，并且电力设计院为开拓总承包业务市场，常常采取低价
竞争等方式，在项目的前期阶段和实施阶段都没有对费用风险引起足够的重视，缺失对
项目的费用风险管理。本文主要是在电力设计院EPC 总承包模式下，对项目全过程进行
费用风险管理，在前期及实施期间进行费用风险识别、分析，对各阶段的决策和管理提
供一定的依据和参考。 
本文首先分析了电力设计院大力发展EPC 总承业务模式的背景，及在此形势下进行
费用风险管理的必要性。分析了电力设计院发展总承包模式的结构及特征，提出了目前
电力设计院费用控制的不足，加强费用风险管理的必要性。总结了项目风险管理的基本
概念和管理过程，归纳了现有的风险识别和分析的方法，以及风险规划策略。 
再利用项目风险识别原理，对项目前期费用风险进行识别，构建了针对总承包项目
前期的费用风险评价指标体系，并构建了基于三角白化权灰色聚类评估的灰色层次评估
模型，利用该方法可以得到总承包项目前期的费用风险整体评价系数，对前期项目管理
者的决策提供参考依据。 
最后依据总承包项目实施过程中费用风险的特点，提出项目实施过程中的费用风险
管理的流程，并对总承包项目实施期的费用风险进行识别，构建了实施阶段费用风险指
标体系，分析了各类风险指标的特点，结合实际工作经验总结各类风险因素的应对策略，
使管理者全面的掌握项目实施过程中存在的费用风险，加强项目管理者的风险意识，并
为管理者制定相应的风险应对策略提供了科学分析方法及理论参考依据。 
 
关键词：电力设计院；EPC 总承包；费用风险；灰色层次分析法 
 
 华北电力大学硕士学位论文 
II 
 
Abstract 
With the deepening of the market economic system the EPC mode was striving develped 
by the electric power design institute. The design institute play a leading role of equipment 
procurement, construction and operation of services to improve the upstream advantages. 
However, the EPC project management of electric power design institute is still in the 
extensive mode right now. The electric power design institute usually adopt "low-cost" 
competition , in order to occupy market share. The project cost risk was not attracted enough 
attention for the EPC project management. This thesis analyze the cost risk in the whole 
process of the EPC project mode, analyze the cost risk for Pre - project and Project 
implementation period. The thesis provide the basis for the decision-making and management 
of the various stages of the project. 
Firstly this thesis introduces the background of the develop of EPC project for the 
electric power design institute , and the necessity of the cost risk management. Summarizes 
the structure and characteristics of EPC development contract mode, puts forward the 
insufficiency of cost control and strengthen the cost risk management of electric power design 
institute. The basic concept and management process of project risk management are 
expounded. The existing risk identification and analysis method and the risk planning method 
are introduced emphatically. 
Then, based on the principle of project risk identification, this thesis constructs a cost 
risk evaluation index system for pre-project cost risk identification, and puts forward a gray 
hierarchy evaluation model based on t riangular whitening right gray clustering evaluation 
model, which can get overall evaluation coefficient of project cost risk, which can provide 
reference for the decision of the project manager. 
Finally, according to the characteristics of the cost risk in the process of the EPC project, 
the thesis puts forward the cost risk management plan in the process of project 
implementation, identifies the cost risk and constructs the cost risk index system in the 
implementation stage. According to the characteristics of various risk indicators, Combined 
with practical work experience to sum up the risk factors coping strategies, so that managers 
have a comprehensive grasp of the project implementation process of the cost risk, strengthen 
the project manager's risk awareness. And provides the scientific analysis method and the 
theory reference basis for the manager to formulate the corresponding risk coping strategy. 
 
Keywords: Electric power design institute， EPC general contracting， Cost risk， Gray 
analytical Hierarchy Process

本论文属于 
  
I 
华 中 科 技 大 学 硕 士 学 位 论 文 
摘  要 
自1984 年国务院开始出台文件提出推行工程总承包建设项目以来，经过30 年
的发展，凭借其缩减工程建设周期、提高工程建设安全、提高工程建设质量和降低
工程成本等优点，被国内工程领域广泛采用。但是在工程总承包模式快速发展的同
时，其弊端也日益凸显，产生的工程纠纷也日益增加，而工程总承包合同风险分担
的不合理性正是导致工程纠纷的根本原因之一。2011 年，我国《建设项目工程总承
包合同示范文本（试行）》颁布，弥补了我国工程总承包市场30 年来合同范本的空
白。但是，由于我国工程总承包合同范本试行时间短，真正应用工程总承包范本为
模板签订的工程项目还不多，国内学者对该合同范本的研究也较少。本文以此为契
机，探讨符合我国工程总承包模式的工程总承包合同风险分担方式。 
首先，本文选择《FIDIC 设计采购施工（EPC）/交钥匙工程合同条件》（1999 年
版）与《建设项目工程总承包合同示范文本（试行）》为研究对象，分析两本合同范
本的特点。其次，在理论基础的研究上，将合同风险分为三级风险，归纳和梳理两
本合同范本三级合同风险，构建合同的风险分担框架。再次，本文从风险管理和补
偿的角度，详细对比国内外工程总承包合同范本的三级合同风险的风险分担差异及
产生差异的原因，提出在我国环境下选择合理的合同分担方式的建议和完善工程总
承包合同范本的建议。最后，以孟加拉国孟巴矿项目案例对合同风险分担的重要性
与合理性予以验证。 
 
关键词：工程总承包   合同范本   风险分担   比较研究   FIDIC 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
  
II 
华 中 科 技 大 学 硕 士 学 位 论 文 
Abstract 
Since the EPC project being advocated by the State Cuncil in 1984, it has been 
achieved a great success after three decades development. However, EPC’s fast 
development also makes its disadvantages become increasingly prominent and the 
unreasonable risk allocation mechanism is one of the many reasons that cause the 
increasing cases of disputes in engineering construction area. In 2011, the government 
issued “Conditions of Contract for EPC (model version)”. This new standard conditions of 
contract fills the gap of no guidance for contract for our General Contracting Market in 
more than thirty years. However, since this new standard conditions of contract just 
carried out in a short period, only a few engineering projects are contracted using the 
guidance for contract of General Contracting Market and few researchers dedicate to 
studying and developing the standard conditions of contract. This thesis dedicates to study 
the best way for our nation’s General Contracting Market’s risk sharing mechanism. 
First of all, “Conditions of Contract for EPC (model version)” and the “Conditions of 
Contract for EPC /Turnkey Projects” are chosen as study subjects, and then their 
specialties are analyzed respectively. Moreover, in order to establish the risk allocation 
framework, contract risk allocation will be firstly classified into three categories and then 
all the two study subjects’ contract risk allocation classifications are identified and 
summarized in terms of the theoretical study. Furthermore, this thesis makes a detailed 
comparison of the difference of risk allocation of those three risk allocation classifications 
between domestic standard conditions of contract and international standard conditions of 
contract and the reasons that are responsible for that from the risk management and 
compensation perspective. It also proposes a reliable way of contract risk allocation 
mechanism and a matured suggestion for our nation’s General Contracting Market’s 
standard conditions of contract. Last but not the least, Bangladesh mining project is used 
to illustrate the reasonability and the importance of risk sharing of contract. 
 
Key words：EPC   Standard Conditions of Contract   Risk Allocation  
    Comparative Study   FIDIC

  西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
EPC 工程总承包项目成本管理探究
专
业：建筑与土木工程
硕士生：陈春苗
指导教师：胡长明教授
摘
要
随着我国经济结构不断调整改革，建筑业也随之不断提升发展，EPC 工程总
承包模式因其特有优势在国内被大力推行。鉴于我国多数建筑企业成本控制思路
不明确，尚未形成一个有效且完善的成本管控体系，导致项目实施过程中存在诸
多成本管理问题与争端。成本管理作为EPC 工程总承包项目管理的核心要素，是
提效益、促发展的必要条件。因此，明晰成本管控重点，选择有效且贴合项目实
际需求的成本管理方法，是EPC 模式发展的关键。综上本文就此展开相关研究。
（1）本文依托A 公司开展的EPC 工程总承包项目作为研究对象。结合EPC
工程项目进行分析，通过文献研究法、案例分析法识别EPC 项目成本影响因素，
借助专家咨询法筛选优化项目成本影响因素体系。基于该成本影响因素体系，以
获得EPC 工程总承包项目成本管理关键因素的影响程度为目标，利用层次分析法
对各个影响因素进行权重计算并比较，以模糊综合评价法修正并比较，最终得到
成本影响因素评价模型与结果。
（2）结合项目实例和成本影响因素分析，综合考量项目成本特征及成本管理
关键因素的影响程度，进而从项目管理、设计及施工三方面提出EPC 工程总承包
项目实施全过程的成本管控措施及建议。
围绕上述研究背景和研究方法，结合案例研究，旨在为EPC 工程总承包项目
成本管理提供新的研究方法和管理思路，以期进一步规范工程总承包成本管理流
程，为提高成本控制效率提供现实参考路径。
关键词：EPC 工程总承包；影响因素；成本管控
 西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
Research on Cost Management of EPC Engineering
General Contract Project
Specialty: Architecture and civil engineering
Name: Chen Chunmiao
Instructor: Prof. Hu Changming
Abstract
With the continuous adjustment and reform of China’s economic structure, the
construction industry is also improving and developing, and the EPC project general
contracting mode has been vigorously promoted in China due to its unique advantages.
It is notable that the cost control methodology of many construction enterprises in
China are not clear enough, and an effective and efficient cost control system has yet to
be formed, leading to many cost management problems and disputes in the process of
project implementation. As a core element of EPC project management, sound cost
management is a necessity to improve efficiency and promote development. Therefore,
it is key to the development of the EPC mode to identifying the focus of cost control
and choosing an effective cost management method that fits the actual needs of the
project. This thesis carries out related research focusing on this issue.
(1) This article relies on the EPC project general contracting project carried out by
a company as the research object. Analysis in the context of EPC project. The
influencing factors of the EPC project cost are identified through literature research
and case analysis, and expert consultation is used to screen and optimize the system of
influencing factors of project cost. Based on the cost influencing factor system, the
thesis takes obtaining the impact degree of the key factors of EPC engineering general
contracting project cost management as the goal, uses the analytic hierarchy process to
calculate and compare the weight of each influencing factor, modifies and compares
with the fuzzy comprehensive evaluation method, and finally get the cost evaluation
model and results of influencing factors.
(2) The thesis combines project examples and cost influencing factor analysis,
comprehensively considers project cost characteristics and the degree of impact of key
 西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
cost management factors, and then proposes cost control measures for and suggestions
on the entire process of EPC project implementation from three aspects: project
management, design and construction.
Based on the above background and methodology, and combined with case study,
the thesis aims to provide new research methods and management ideas for the cost
management of EPC engineering general contract project, in order to further
standardize the cost management process of engineering general contract project, and
provide realistic reference for improving the cost control efficiency.
Key words: EPC general contracting project; affect factors; cost control

  西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
EPC 模式下M 钢铁公司炼钢项目风险管理研究
专
业：项目管理
研究生：杨云周
指导老师：宋宏副教授
摘
要
冶金建设项目具有投资规模较大、建设周期偏长，设备供货和施工材料总投
资占比高，设计和设备供货周期较长，施工周期、安装调试周期较长的显著特点，
因此项目执行过程中在质量、安全、进度、成本等方面存在不确定性管理风险。
EPC 项目管理模式所具有的优势，使得EPC 管理模式在冶金建设项目中得到普遍
的应用。但如何识别这些风险并根据风险的种类和发生的概率制定相应的对策，
成为冶金建设项目总承包项目管理中不容回避的一个重要问题。
本文在冶金建设项目特点进行研究的基础上，结合EPC 总承包项目的实施过
程，提出了冶金建设总承包项目风险评价的方法，建立了风险评价指标体系和评
价模型。以M 钢铁公司炼钢总承包项目为例，建立了包含8 个一级指标23 个二
级指标的风险评价指标体系。通过专家调研的方法确定了M 钢铁公司炼钢总承包
项目二级指标的风险概率分布，采用CIM 并联响应模型计算了一级指标的风险概
率分布。利用层次分析法（AHP）计算各指标权重的基础上，评价了该项目的风
险，明确了对项目具有重要影响的风险要素和风险概率较高的指标，提出了风险
防范的措施。
经过分析计算，M 钢铁公司炼钢厂建设项目采用EPC 总承包模式，能够满足
项目建设的基本要求，但其风险分析的结果为“较高”，需要采取有效的风险控制
措施加以防范。在各风险要素中，进度风险、成本风险概率相对较高，是风险控
制和防范的关键因素。
关键词：EPC 管理模式；冶金建设项目；风险分析；CIM；AHP
 西安建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
Research on Risk Management of M Steel Corp Steelmaking
Project under EPC Mode
Specialty: Project Management
Name: Yang Yunzhou
Instructor: Associate Prof. Song Hong
Abstract
Metallurgical
Construction
Project
with
large
scale
of
investment,
long
construction period, construction materials and equipment supply total investment ratio,
design and equipment supply cycle longer, obvious characteristics of long cycle cycle
installation and construction, the project implementation process of uncertainty
management risks in terms of quality, safety, schedule, cost etc.. EPC project
management model has the advantages of EPC management model in the construction
of the project has been widely used. However, how to identify these risks and
formulate corresponding countermeasures according to the types of risks and the
probability of occurrence has become an important problem that can not be avoided in
the EPC project management.
On basis of research on characteristics of metallurgical construction projects and
integrating it with the implementation process of metallurgical projects, this paper
proposes the method of risk assessment of metallurgical construction projects and
establishes a risk evaluation index system and evaluation model. Taking the
steelmaking plant project of M Steel Corp as an example, a risk assessment index
system is established, which includes 8 primary level indexes and 23 secondary level
indexes. The probability distribution of the two indexes of the general contract project
of M Steel Corp steelmaking plant is determined by expert investigation, and that of
the primary level indexes is calculated by using the CIM parallel response model.
Based on the weight calculation of each index by using AHP method, the project risks
are assessed, the risk factors and the risk of higher probability which have important
influence on the project are identifies, and risk prevention measures are put forward.
On basis of calculation and analysis, to implement M the Steel Corp’s steel plant
 西建筑科技大学硕士学位论文
project under the EPC general contracting model can meet the basic requirements of
project construction, but the result of the risk analysis is "high"; effective measures
shall be taken for risk control. Among the risk factors, the risks of project schedule and
cost are relatively high and therefore are the key factors of risk control and prevention.
Keywords: EPC management mode, Metallurgical industrial Project, Risk analysis,
CIM, AHP

of

management for metallurgical engineering projects
作者姓名：王波

学科专业名称：工程管理（MEM）

  III
摘
要
目前，我国冶金行业上马了较多的工业工程，这些项目中很大一部分都采取了EPC
这一先进管理模式，也带来了一定的风险。部分的EPC 总承包商专业技术与管理能力有
所欠缺，加上对风险的认识与管理不到位，就极容易产生风险事件，导致项目的建设出
现了困难。风险控制理论认为风险对于人类生产以及生活可能会造成损害，一定需要提
前予以控制。风险控制应在科学理论指导下，制定出全面的风险控制规划，采用科学的
控制技术，按照合理的流程进行风险的管理。
为了扩大再生产，山东钢铁集团球墨铸铁管公司经过研究，决定兴建搬迁项目（以
下简称山东铁管公司搬迁项目），将生产部分基地搬迁到临沂市兰陵县的矿业循环经济
工业园区。山东铁管公司搬迁项目EPC 总承包管理工作包含设计、设备采购、施工、安
装调试、热负荷试车、试运行、完成功能考核、人员培训、技术服务直至竣工验收合格，
以及缺陷修复、在质量保证期内的工程质量保证全过程的管理。管理工作内容多，程序
复杂，这使得山东铁管公司搬迁项目EPC 总承包管理具有较多的风险。研究中，以山东
铁管公司搬迁项目EPC 总承包管理的风险控制为研究对象，按照风险控制流程，运用WBS
和流程图分析法等技术方法来识别风险，确定了山东铁管公司搬迁项目EPC 总承包管理
的11 项风险；采取风险矩阵图法来评价风险，研究确定出严重风险、一般风险和较小
风险；并以此为依据，有针对性地制定风险处置方法，落实风险处置措施；在风险控制
过程中，还实施开展风险监测，确保随时掌握风险的状态，保证了冶金工程项目EPC
总承包管理的风险得到了有效的控制。
关键词：冶金工程项目
EPC
风险控制流程图分析法风险矩阵图法
 IV
Abstract
At present, there are more industrial projects has launched in China's metallurgical
industry, a large part of these projects have adopted EPC this advanced management model,
but also brought certain risks. Some EPC general contractors lack of professional technology
and management capabilities, coupled with inadequate risk awareness and management, it is
extremely easy to produce risk incidents, resulting in difficulties in the construction of the
project. Risk controlling theory holds that risk may cause damage to human production and
life and must be controlled in advance. Risk control should be guided by scientific theory,
formulate a comprehensive risk control plan, adopt scientific controlling technology, and
carry out risk management according to a reasonable process.
In order to expand the reproduction, Shandong Metallurgical Group Ductile Iron Pipe
Company has decided to build a relocation project of Shandong Ductile Iron Pipe Company,
relocating some production bases to the mining circular economy industrial park of Lanling
County, Linyi City. The EPC general contracting management of the relocation project of
Shandong Ductile Iron Pipe Company includes design, equipment procurement, construction,
installation and commissioning, thermal load test, commissioning, completion of functional
assessment, personnel training, technical services until the completion of acceptance, and
defect repair,during the quality assurance period, the whole process management of
engineering quality assurance . There are many management contents and complicated
procedures, which make the relocation project EPC general contract management of
Shandong ductile iron pipe company have more risks. In the study, the risk control of EPC
general contracting management in the relocation project of Shandong Ductile Iron Pipe
Company was taken as the research object, According to the risk control process, using WBS
and flow chart analysis method to identify risks, 11 risks of EPC general contracting
management of relocation project of Shandong Ductile Iron Pipe Company were determined；
risk matrix diagram method was used to evaluate risks, and serious risks, general risks and
minor risks were determined, On this basis, risk disposal methods are formulated and risk
disposal measures are implemented. In the process of risk control, risk monitoring is carried
out to ensure that the state of risk is grasped at any time, and the risk of EPC general
contracting management of metallurgical engineering projects is effectively controlled.
Key words:
Metallurgical Engineering Project
EPC
Risk Management
Flow chart analysis method
Risk matrix method

  摘要
1
摘要
近些年，风电能源在全世界迅速发展，引起了全球普遍高度的重视，其
发展呈现了强劲发展态势。统计资料表明，风电能源的年增长率接近27%。
作为一种新兴能源，它的发展将成为以后的重点。目前，中国的经济进一步
高速进步和发展，对电力供应的需求进一步扩大，风电能源进入了一个良好
的发展机遇期，中国已经成为世界上风力发电装机最多的国家之一。近年来，
受我国政策大力支持和世界需求增加因素影响，我国风电生产能力一直保持
着良好的发展势头。据行业统计，2018 年，新增并网风电装机2059 万千瓦，
累计并网装机容量达到1.84 亿千瓦，是全球范围内的第一个装机达到一亿千
瓦的国家。风电行业的迅速发展将极大地促进风电项目市场的发展壮大，同
时为国内的风电项目承包商带来了更多的机会。
随着新能源政策的不断推广，风能已成为我国当前大力发展的洁净能源
结构的有机组成部分。EPC 是一种新型项目管理模型，它被广泛运用于风电
建设中。对投资者来说，这种模式兼具成本比较低、稳定性比较好的优点，
但也有很大的风险。为此，降低和规避EPC 模式下风能建设项目施工中潜在
的风险，进一步强化EPC 模式下风能项目的风控显得至关重要。本研究结合
国内外流行的风险管理前沿理论，对四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目业主风险
进行识别、分析与评估，通过对四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目业主风险进行
实证分析，在此基础上，本文提出了针对四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目业主
风险的监测措施，本文的主要结论有以下三个方面：
第一，四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目中，投资者风险可进一步细分成三
类：投资方风险、政策风险和建筑施工风险。在项目的整个过程中，这些风
险因素可以全面地、具体地、科学地反映出四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目业
主风险的各个方面。
 四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目业主风险管理研究
2
第二，本文借助模糊综合评价模型，以四川省A 风电EPC 总承包项目作
为研究对象，实际调查、评价结果和风险因素分析表明，四川省A 风电EPC
总承包项目的主要风险来自业主自身和工程方面、合同管理风险、财务管理
风险、法律责任风险和相关政策法规不配套风险，这些是对EPC 模式项目业
主最具影响力的风险因素。
第三，针对业主的风险管理措施，本文提出了四川省A 风电EPC 总承包
项目可能遭遇的各类潜在风险以及解决方案，投资者可结合项目本身特点，
科学合理地选择并加以运用，从而执行有效的风险管理计划。
关键词：风电项目；EPC 总承包；业主风险；风险控制
 摘要
1
Abstract
In recent years, the rapid development of wind power in the world has
attracted worldwide attention, and its development shows a strong trend of
development. Statistical data show that the annual growth rate of wind power is
close to 27%. As a new energy, its development will become the focus in the
future. At present, China's economy has made further rapid progress and
development, and the demand for power supply has further expanded. Wind power
has entered a good period of development opportunities. China has become one of
the countries with the largest installed wind power in the world. In recent years,
driven by national policies and the global trend of development, China's wind
power continues to maintain a high-speed development momentum. In 2018,
China's new installed wind power reached 20.59 million kilowatts. The cumulative
installed capacity of grid-connected wind power reached 184 million kilowatts,
which is the first installed capacity in the world. To 100 million kilowatt countries.
The rapid development of the wind power industry will greatly promote the
development and growth of the wind power project market, while bringing more
opportunities for domestic wind power project contractors.
With the gradual advancement of new energy policy, wind energy has
become an important part of the new energy structure. As an excellent project
management model, EPC model has been widely used in wind farm construction
projects. For owners, this management model has the advantages of low cost and
good stability, but it also has great risks. Therefore, it is necessary to reduce and
avoid the risks in the construction process of wind energy construction projects
under EPC mode and strengthen the risk management of wind energy construction
projects under EPC mode. Based on the risk management theory, this paper
identifies, analyses and evaluates the owner risk of Sichuan A wind power EPC
general contract project. Through the empirical analysis of the owner risk of
Sichuan A wind power EPC general contract project, on this basis, this paper puts
 Study on Owner Risk Management of A Wind Power EPC General Contracting Project in Sichuan Province
2
forward the monitoring measures for the owner risk of Sichuan A wind power EPC
general contract project. The main conclusions are as follows:
Firstly, the owner risk of Sichuan A wind power EPC general contracting
project can be divided into three categories: owner's own risk, policy system risk
and construction risk. In the whole process of the project, these risk factors can
comprehensively, concretely and scientifically reflect all aspects of the owner's
risk of Sichuan A wind power EPC general contracting project.
Secondly, with the help of the fuzzy comprehensive evaluation model, this
paper takes Sichuan A wind power EPC general contracting project as an example.
The evaluation results and actual investigation and risk factor analysis show that
the main risks of Sichuan A wind power EPC general contracting project come
from owners themselves and engineering aspects, contract management risks,
financial management risks and legal responsibilities. Allowance risk and
incompatible risk of relevant policies and regulations are the most influential risk
factors for EPC mode project owners.
Thirdly, aiming at the risk management measures of the owners, this paper
puts forward a reference response plan of various risk factors faced by Sichuan A
wind power EPC general contracting project. Taking into account the specific
characteristics of such projects the owners can choose and use it scientifically and
reasonably, so as to implement effective risk management plan.
Keywords: Wind Power Project; EPC General Contracting; Owner Risk;
Risk Control

## P000027 (分9 50,037字 crosspool:EPC-CONTRACT unknown T1:0)
路径: 鞍钢集团矿业设计研究院EPC工程总承包管理模式案例研究.pdf

 
  
  
  
 
 
 
 
A Case Study of Engineering General Contracting 
Management Mode of EPC for Angang Ming Engineering 
Company 
 
 
 
by 
Zhao Man 
（Majoring in Master of Business Administration） 
 
 
Supervisor : Prof. Wang Yadong 
 
 
 
 
May 5，2014
 摘 要 
I 
摘  要 
EPC 工程总承包管理模式是目前国际工程承包市场上一种流行和有效的工
程项目管理模式，也是我国建设部门大力推广的一种承发包模式。所谓EPC 工
程总承包是指从事工程总承包的企业受业主委托，按照合同约定对工程项目的设
计、采购、施工（安装）、试运行（竣工验收）阶段等实行全过程或若干阶段的
承包。随着三十年的发展，EPC 总承包市场在国内逐步走向成熟，并从最初的建
筑业逐步进入了更多的领域，成为工程项目管理的首选模式。由于国家鼓励有能
力的勘察设计企业开展EPC 工程总承包，使得国内的勘察设计企业纷纷进军总
承包市场，不断培养和积累作为工程总承包商的能力和经验。 
鞍钢集团矿业设计研究院是目前全国唯一专业从事冶金矿山科研、设计、勘
察、监理的工程技术咨询机构，是鞍山钢铁集团公司的三大研发机构之一，具有
冶金、矿山、建筑、规划、建材及压力管道等多行业资质，有多项技术和设计成
果获得国家级、省部级等奖项，能够承担特大型、大型冶金矿山从采选工艺研究、
技术经济论证、可行性研究到工程勘察、设计、监理和工程总承包等全套业务。
作为国内冶金行业知名的勘察设计企业，近年来该院不断拓宽业务领域和范围，
探索EPC 工程总承包管理模式，在实践中取得了良好的效果。 
本文首先介绍了冶金勘查设计企业EPC工程总承包管理模式的背景及意义，
其次以该院开展的“攀钢新白马及及坪采场矿石破碎及胶带运输工程”为实例，
阐述了EPC 工程总承包管理模式的实际运行情况，再次追述了EPC 工程总承包
的概念、适用范围、主要内容、流程、特征、优势、风险等内容，并分析了AMEC
开展EPC 工程总承包的原因、优势以及可能性和必要性，最后总结了该项目实
施过程中取得的经验和教训，并对开展过程中存在的问题提出了应对的建议，为
该院今后开展类似总承包业务提供借鉴，也为相关专业企业推行该模式提供借
鉴。 
 
关键词：EPC 工程总承包；管理模式；冶金行业；勘察设计企业 
 
 
 Abstract 
 
II
Abstract 
EPC general contract management mode is a popular and effective project 
management mode in the international project contracting market ,it is also a mode 
that China's construction sector to promote. The so-called EPC general contract is 
general contracting company that engaged in the construction who commissioned by 
the owners, in accordance with the contract, to deal with design, project procurement, 
construction （installation）, test run （acceptance） stage of the whole process or 
some stages of the contractor. With thirty years development, EPC general contract in 
domestic market is gradually mature, and from the initial building industry gradually 
into more areas, has become the preferred mode of construction project management. 
The State encourages exploration and design enterprises have the capacity to carry out 
EPC project general contracting, the prospecting and design enterprises of China have 
entered the general contracting market, and has cultivated and accumulated as 
contractor's ability and experience. 
Anshan Iron and Steel Group Mining Engineering Company is currently the only 
specialized in metallurgical and mining research, design, investigation, supervision of 
engineering consulting agencies, Anshan Iron And Steel Group Corporation is one of 
the three R & D institutions, metallurgy, mine, construction, planning, building 
materials and pressure piping and other multi industry qualification, there are a 
number of technical and design results obtained national, provincial and ministerial 
level awards, it can undertake  oversized large metallurgical mines from mining 
technology research, technical and economic evaluation, feasibility study to project 
survey, design, supervision and construction general contracting a full set of business. 
As a famous domestic metallurgical industry prospecting and design company, in 
recent years, this company expanded its business scope, exploration of EPC general 
contract management mode, and achieved good results in practice. 
This paper firstly introduces the background and significance of metallurgical 
exploration of general contract management mode design of enterprise EPC project, 
followed by this company who carry out the "Pangang new BaiMa and JijiPing stope 
ore crushing and tape transport engineering" for example, expound the actual 
operation situation of EPC general contract management mode, once again discussed 
the concept, EPC contractor of applicable scope, main content, process, characteristics, 
advantages, risk and so on, and analyzed the reason of carrying out EPC project 
general contracting, the advantages of AMEC and the possibility and necessity, 
 Abstract 
III 
finally the implementation of the project experience and lessons in the process, and 
the suggestions for the existing problems in the development process, in order to 
provide reference for the future similar general contracting business, also provides the 
reference for the implementation of the relevant professional model enterprises. 
 
Key Words: EPC general contract, management mode, metallurgical enterprise, 
prospecting and design company
